# 🎨 AKR / Styles — Component Library Catalog

A curated, production-ready design system bundle scraped from **styles.refero.design**. Features **1,290 total component design specifications**, complete with Tailwind CSS v4 themes, native CSS custom properties, W3C Design Tokens, design spec markdown files, and direct HD MP4 video previews.

## ⚡ Interactive Component Gallery

> 💡 **Tip:** You can view, search, filter, and inspect code for all 1,290 components visually in your web browser!
> Simply open [`index.html`](file:///home/prince/ProjectsMain/akr-inspo-component-library%20%281%29/Refero-styles%20design%20dna%20scrape/index.html) or host it locally/on GitHub Pages to access real-time search, live `.mp4` playback, theme filters, and one-click code copy.

## 📁 Folder Structure Per Component

Each component inside `./refero_bundle/<slug>-<uuid8>/` contains the following assets:

```text
refero_bundle/<component-slug>/
├── preview.mp4          # 🎬 HD Interaction preview video
├── DESIGN.md            # 📖 Aesthetic spec, vibe, color rationale & typography
├── tailwind-v4.css      # 🎨 Tailwind CSS v4 @theme block
├── css-variables.css    # ⚙️ Standard CSS custom properties (:root)
├── design-tokens.json   # 💎 W3C-formatted JSON Design Tokens
└── source.txt           # 🔗 Original Refero style source URL
```

## 🚀 How to Use These Components

### 1. Tailwind CSS v4 Integration
Open `tailwind-v4.css` from any component directory and paste the `@theme` block directly into your global stylesheet (e.g. `main.css` or `globals.css`):

```css
@import "tailwindcss";

/* Paste theme block from component's tailwind-v4.css */
@theme {
  --color-brand-blue: #145fe4;
  --color-deep-space: #1d1d21;
}
```

### 2. Standard CSS Custom Properties
Import or copy `css-variables.css` into your CSS reset or root styles:

```css
/* Import in your root HTML/CSS */
@import "./refero_bundle/1password-da0bfca3/css-variables.css";

.my-card {
  background-color: var(--color-deep-space);
  color: var(--color-brand-blue);
}
```

### 3. W3C Design Tokens (`design-tokens.json`)
Use `design-tokens.json` directly with [Style Dictionary](https://amzn.github.io/style-dictionary/), Figma Token Sync, or custom build scripts to output tokens for iOS, Android, Web, or Flutter.

## 🛠️ Maintainer & Re-Scraping Instructions

If you need to update or re-scrape components from Refero Styles:

```bash
# Install dependencies
uv sync --extra render
uv run playwright install chromium

# Run scraper (concurrent workers)
uv run main.py --out ./refero_bundle --workers 4

# Regenerate catalog README & HTML Viewer
python3 build_catalog.py
```
